    """The answer of this Codex process for this entry: the first run records its folder and its answer; a later run
    gives the header only when the first one did, in the same folder, and this one would too. Any `{}` stays. Deny by
    default: a run with no record counts as the first only while the process is younger than FIRST_WINDOW, so a first
    run that failed (a busy lock, the alarm) never lets a later run in another folder count as first, and neither
    does a record that expired (Gate A and Gate B review, round 2). A file that cannot be read or written gives no
    header."""
    lock = runs_file + ".lock"
    deadline = time.time() + 1.0
    while True:
        try:
            os.mkdir(lock, 0o700)
            break
        except FileExistsError:
            try:
                if time.time() - os.stat(lock).st_mtime > LOCK_STALE:
                    os.rmdir(lock)
                    continue
            except OSError:
                continue
            if time.time() > deadline:
                return False
            time.sleep(0.02)
        except OSError:
            return False
    try:
        try:
            with open(runs_file, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                runs = json.load(f)
        except FileNotFoundError:
            runs = {}
        except (OSError, ValueError, RecursionError):
            return False
        runs = runs if isinstance(runs, dict) else {}
        now = time.time()
        rec = runs.get(proc)
        runs = {k: v for k, v in runs.items() if isinstance(v, dict) and isinstance(v.get("t"), (int, float))
                and now - v["t"] < RUN_TTL}
        if not isinstance(rec, dict) or not isinstance(rec.get("t"), (int, float)):
            ok = fresh and 0 <= age < FIRST_WINDOW
            runs[proc] = {"cwd": folder, "ok": ok, "t": now}
        else:
            ok = bool(rec.get("ok")) and rec.get("cwd") == folder and fresh
            runs[proc] = {"cwd": rec.get("cwd"), "ok": ok, "t": rec["t"]}
        tmp = f"{runs_file}.{os.getpid()}.tmp"
        fd = os.open(tmp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0), 0o600)
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(runs, f)
        os.replace(tmp, runs_file)
        return ok
    except OSError:
        return False
    finally:
        try:
            os.rmdir(lock)
        except OSError:
            pass


POLICY_FILES = {"darwin": "/Library/Application Support/maisecrets/policy.json", "linux": "/etc/maisecrets/policy.json"}


def switched_off(home: str) -> bool:
    """`client_header` false in the machine policy (it wins) or in the maisecrets config.json, read at each run: the
    opt-out works at the next connection, before any hook of that start ran (Fable review). A file that exists and
    cannot be read or parsed counts as off."""
    policy = POLICY_FILES.get(sys.platform if sys.platform != "linux2" else "linux")
    for path in (policy, os.path.join(home, "config.json")):
        if not path or not os.path.exists(path):
            continue
        try:
            with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
        except (OSError, ValueError, RecursionError):
            return True
        if not isinstance(data, dict):
            return True
        if "client_header" in data:
            return data["client_header"] is False
    return False


def entry_header(state_file: str, codex_home: str, name: str, url: str, folder: str) -> str | None:
    """The header value when every check of this run holds, without the record of the process; or None."""
    if switched_off(os.path.dirname(os.path.dirname(state_file))):
        return None
    try:
        with open(state_file, encoding="utf-8") as f:
            state = json.load(f)
    except (OSError, ValueError, RecursionError):
        return None
    profile = os.path.realpath(os.path.join(codex_home, "config.toml"))
    mine = state.get(profile) if isinstance(state, dict) else None
    rec = mine.get(name) if isinstance(mine, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(rec, dict) or rec.get("url") != url:
        return None
    header = rec.get("header")
    if not isinstance(header, str) or not _HEADER_RE.fullmatch(header):
        return None
    if not user_entry_is_plain(load(profile), name, url) or profile_files_bring_servers(codex_home):
        return None
    if project_brings_servers(folder, codex_home):
        return None
    return header


def header_for(state_file: str, codex_home: str, name: str, url: str, folder: str) -> str | None:
    """The header for this entry in this run, or None."""
    header = entry_header(state_file, codex_home, name, url, folder)
    found = codex_process()
    if found is None:
        return None
    proc, age = found
    runs = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(state_file), "codex-helper-runs.json")
    key = f"{proc}\0{os.path.realpath(os.path.join(codex_home, 'config.toml'))}\0{name}"
    return header if first_answer(runs, key, folder, header is not None, age) else None


def _answer(header: str | None) -> None:
    os.write(1, json.dumps({HEADER: header} if header else {}).encode())


def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
    if hasattr(os, "fork"):
        import signal

        def late(*_a) -> None:
            _answer(None)
            os._exit(0)
        signal.signal(signal.SIGALRM, late)
        signal.alarm(DEADLINE)
    header = None
    try:
        if len(argv) == 4:
            header = header_for(argv[0], argv[1], argv[2], argv[3], os.getcwd())
    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 - a helper that fails stops the server: never
        header = None
    if hasattr(os, "fork"):
        import signal
        signal.alarm(0)
    _answer(header)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

"""Whether Codex runs the hooks of this plugin folder, read from Codex's own config (read only).

Codex runs a plugin hook only while the trust the person gave it matches the hook. Per hook it keeps, in config.toml,
`[hooks.state."<plugin id>:hooks/hooks.json:<event>:<group>:<handler>"]`:
- `trusted_hash`: the hook's declaration (event, matcher, command, timeout; codex-rs hooks/src/engine/discovery.rs);
- `trusted_content_hash`, from Codex 0.164 on: the contents of the whole plugin folder
  (hooks/src/engine/plugin_fingerprint.rs, openai/codex#54619). A new maisecrets version is a new folder, so Codex
  asks again after each update, and until then it lets every prompt and tool call through (measured with
  codex-cli 0.164.0-alpha.4, 2026-10-19).
No hook can say that it does not run, so /maisecrets:status says it. Both hashes are recomputed here; the values
Codex itself gave for a fixture plugin pin the recipe (tests/test_codex_trust.py).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import stat
import struct
from pathlib import Path

EVENTS = {"SessionStart": "session_start", "UserPromptSubmit": "user_prompt_submit",
          "PreToolUse": "pre_tool_use", "PostToolUse": "post_tool_use"}
_SKIPPED = ("__pycache__", ".venv")


def declaration_hash(event: str, group: dict, handler: dict, windows: bool) -> str:
    command = (handler.get("commandWindows") or handler["command"]) if windows else handler["command"]
    hook = {"type": "command", "command": command, "timeout": handler.get("timeout", 600), "async": False}
    if handler.get("statusMessage") is not None:
        hook["statusMessage"] = handler["statusMessage"]   # part of the declaration (codex-rs config/hook_config.rs)
    identity = {"event_name": EVENTS[event], "hooks": [hook]}
    if group.get("matcher") is not None:
        identity["matcher"] = group["matcher"]
    blob = json.dumps(identity, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), ensure_ascii=False).encode("utf-8")
    return "sha256:" + hashlib.sha256(blob).hexdigest()


def content_hash(root: Path, windows: bool | None = None) -> str:
    """The plugin folder as Codex fingerprints it: depth first in byte order of the names, without bytecode,
    virtual environments and package metadata. A symlink makes Codex fail the check, and this raises."""
    h = hashlib.sha256(b"codex-plugin-bundle-v1")
    pending = [root]
    while pending:
        path = pending.pop()
        rel = "" if path == root else path.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        name = path.name if rel else ""
        if name in _SKIPPED or name.endswith(".pyc") or name.endswith(".egg-info"):
            continue
        if (os.name == "nt") if windows is None else windows:
            rel = rel.replace("/", "\\")       # as Codex spells it there (measured, windows-latest, 2026-10-19)
        encoded = rel.encode("utf-8", "surrogateescape")
        h.update(struct.pack("<Q", len(encoded)))
        h.update(encoded)
        mode = os.lstat(path).st_mode
        if stat.S_ISDIR(mode):
            h.update(b"directory")
            pending.extend(sorted(path.iterdir(), key=lambda p: os.fsencode(p.name), reverse=True))
        elif stat.S_ISREG(mode):
            data = path.read_bytes()
            h.update(b"file")
            h.update(struct.pack("<Q", len(data)))
            h.update(data)
        else:
            raise OSError(f"Codex does not fingerprint {rel}: not a file or a folder")
    return "sha256:" + h.hexdigest()


def _plugin_id(root: Path, codex_home: Path) -> str | None:
    """<plugin>@<marketplace> for a folder Codex installed: <codex home>/plugins/cache/<marketplace>/<plugin>/<v>."""
    try:
        parts = root.resolve().relative_to((codex_home / "plugins" / "cache").resolve()).parts
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None
    return f"{parts[1]}@{parts[0]}" if len(parts) == 3 else None


def _legacy_baseline(codex_home: Path, plugin_id: str) -> str | None:
    """The contents an approval from before Codex 0.164 is pinned to, at its first run on 0.164 or newer
    (hooks/src/engine/plugin_trust.rs, legacy_trust_path: the real path of config.toml and the plugin id)."""
    config = os.path.realpath(codex_home / "config.toml")
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    for part in (os.fsencode(config), plugin_id.encode("utf-8")):
        h.update(struct.pack("<Q", len(part)))
        h.update(part)
    try:
        return (Path(config).parent / ".hook-trust" / h.hexdigest()).read_text(encoding="utf-8").strip()
    except OSError:
        return None


def _load_toml(path: Path) -> dict | None:
    try:
        import tomllib
    except ImportError:              # Python 3.10 and older
        return None
    try:
        with open(path, "rb") as f:
            return tomllib.load(f)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return None


def status_lines(root: Path, codex_home: Path, windows: bool | None = None) -> list[str]:
    """The lines /maisecrets:status prints for Codex; none for a folder Codex did not install."""
    windows = os.name == "nt" if windows is None else windows
    plugin_id = _plugin_id(root, codex_home)
    if plugin_id is None:
        return []
    cfg = _load_toml(codex_home / "config.toml")
    if cfg is None:
        return ["codex hooks: unknown (needs Python 3.11 or newer to read Codex's config.toml)"]
    out = []
    others = sorted(k for k, v in (cfg.get("plugins") or {}).items()
                    if k.startswith("maisecrets@") and k != plugin_id and (v or {}).get("enabled", True))
    if others:
        out.append(f"codex: another maisecrets is installed too ({', '.join(others)}): remove it with "
                   f"codex plugin remove, so that one version runs the hooks")
    try:
        hooks = json.loads((root / "hooks" / "hooks.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))["hooks"]
        current = content_hash(root, windows)
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError) as e:
        return out + [f"codex hooks: unknown ({type(e).__name__})"]
    state = (cfg.get("hooks") or {}).get("state") or {}
    baseline = _legacy_baseline(codex_home, plugin_id)
    untrusted = changed = old = total = 0
    for event, groups in hooks.items():
        if event not in EVENTS:
            continue
        for gi, group in enumerate(groups):
            for hi, handler in enumerate(group.get("hooks", [])):
                total += 1
                entry = state.get(f"{plugin_id}:hooks/hooks.json:{EVENTS[event]}:{gi}:{hi}") or {}
                if entry.get("trusted_hash") != declaration_hash(event, group, handler, windows):
                    untrusted += 1
                elif entry.get("trusted_content_hash") is None and baseline is None:
                    old += 1
                elif (entry.get("trusted_content_hash") or baseline) != current:
                    changed += 1
    fix = "open /hooks in Codex and trust the maisecrets entries; until then Codex lets prompts and tool calls through"
    warn = "WARNING: maisecrets does not protect Codex now: "
    if untrusted:
        out.append(f"{warn}codex hooks: {untrusted} of {total} not trusted: {fix}")
    elif changed:
        out.append(f"{warn}codex hooks: trusted for other contents of this folder (an update, or a changed file), "
                   f"so Codex 0.164 and newer skip them: {fix}")
    elif old:
        out.append("codex hooks: trusted (an approval from before Codex 0.164: after the next maisecrets update, "
                   "Codex 0.164 and newer ask again in /hooks)")
    else:
        out.append(f"codex hooks: trusted ({total} of {total})")
    return out

"""Vault references in Codex: the client side of vault_refs.py for codex-cli (measured on 0.162.0).

Three facts of Codex shape this module (docs/CLIENTS.md, "Codex: vault references"):

- A repository's `.codex/config.toml` merges deeply over the user's `$CODEX_HOME/config.toml`. A project layer that
  names a user entry can give it another URL, and a static `http_headers` header of that entry then goes to that URL.
  So maisecrets never writes `http_headers`. It writes one `http_headers_helper` line on the user entry: a fixed
  command (codex_helper.py) that Codex runs at connect time in the session folder, and that prints the header only
  when no project layer of that folder has servers (codex_helper.header_for), else `{}`.
- The PostToolUse payload names the tool only (`mcp__<server>__<tool>`), with no server, URL or scope. The server is
  found by Codex's own name rule; a project layer with servers, or a name that two entries could make, notes nothing.
- Codex reads config.toml when it starts: a helper line added in a session works from the next start.

The write changes one line of config.toml and nothing else: a parse before and after must differ in exactly that key
of that entry, or nothing is written. Needs tomllib (Python 3.11+); without it, and on Windows, this module is off.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

from . import codex_helper
from .vault import _DEFAULT_HOME, HOME, _lock_for, atomic_write

HELPER_KEY = "http_headers_helper"
HELPER_FILE = HOME / "bin" / "codex-vault-helper.py"   # outside the plugin folder: an update moves that folder
STATE = HOME / "vault-refs" / "codex-helper.json"       # profile -> name -> {"url", "header"}; the helper reads it
LOCK_STALE = 10.0


def available() -> bool:
    """Codex vault references run here: tomllib exists and this is not Windows (not measured there)."""
    return os.name != "nt" and codex_helper._toml() is not None


def codex_home() -> Path:
    """CODEX_HOME as Codex takes it, not resolved: a linked config.toml says nothing about where the folder is."""
    base = os.environ.get("CODEX_HOME")
    return Path(os.path.abspath(os.path.expanduser(base))) if base else Path.home() / ".codex"


def config_path() -> Path:
    """The user layer of Codex: the only file maisecrets changes."""
    return codex_home() / "config.toml"


def profile() -> str:
    return os.path.realpath(str(config_path()))


def _origin_ok(url: object) -> bool:
    from .vault_refs import origin_of
    return bool(origin_of(url))


def user_servers(data: dict | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
    """name -> URL of each user entry with an https URL (streamable HTTP, not switched off)."""
    if data is None:
        data = codex_helper.load(profile()) or {}
    servers = data.get("mcp_servers")
    if not isinstance(servers, dict):
        return {}
    return {name: spec["url"] for name, spec in servers.items()
            if isinstance(name, str) and isinstance(spec, dict) and "command" not in spec
            and spec.get("enabled", True) is not False and _origin_ok(spec.get("url"))}


SESSIONS = HOME / "vault-refs" / "codex-sessions.json"   # session id -> {"cwd", "t"}: the folder a session began in
SESSION_TTL = 7 * 24 * 3600


def note_session(payload: dict) -> None:
    """At SessionStart: the folder of the session. Codex fixes the server URLs from the project layers of this folder;
    a later turn in another folder keeps them (Gate B review)."""
    session, cwd = payload.get("session_id"), payload.get("cwd")
    if isinstance(session, str) and session and isinstance(cwd, str) and cwd:
        # what Codex merged at this start: a project layer or a profile file with servers. A layer the agent deletes
        # later does not change the URLs of this session, so the record keeps the answer of the start (Fable review)
        home = str(codex_home())
        moved = codex_helper.project_brings_servers(cwd, home) or codex_helper.profile_files_bring_servers(home)
        _first_folder(session, cwd, moved)


def _session_folder(session: str) -> str | None:
    """The folder recorded at the SessionStart of this session, when the start saw no project layer or profile file
    with servers; else None. Only SessionStart records one: a result without it notes nothing, also when the record
    failed or expired (Gate A and Gate B review, round 2), and a record without the answer of the start counts as
    one that saw servers."""
    try:
        data = json.loads(SESSIONS.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError, RecursionError):
        return None
    rec = data.get(session) if isinstance(data, dict) else None
    if not isinstance(rec, dict) or not isinstance(rec.get("cwd"), str) or rec.get("moved") is not False:
        return None
    return rec["cwd"]


def _first_folder(session: str, cwd: str, moved: bool = True) -> str | None:
    """The first folder recorded for this session; this cwd when there is none yet. None when the file is damaged.
    A later SessionStart of the same session (a resume, a new Codex process that merges the layers again) can only
    raise `moved`, never lower it, and so can a start in another folder (Fable review of !94)."""
    SESSIONS.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)     # a first start: the folder is not there yet
    with _lock_for(SESSIONS.parent / ".lock"):
        try:
            data = json.loads(SESSIONS.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except FileNotFoundError:
            data = {}
        except (OSError, ValueError, RecursionError):
            return None
        data = data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
        rec = data.get(session)
        now = time.time()
        data = {k: v for k, v in data.items() if isinstance(v, dict) and isinstance(v.get("t"), (int, float))
                and now - v["t"] < SESSION_TTL}
        if isinstance(rec, dict) and isinstance(rec.get("cwd"), str):
            raised = rec.get("moved") is not False or bool(moved) or rec["cwd"] != cwd
            data[session] = {"cwd": rec["cwd"], "t": now, "moved": raised}
            atomic_write(SESSIONS, json.dumps(data, indent=1))
            return rec["cwd"]
        data[session] = {"cwd": cwd, "t": now, "moved": bool(moved)}
        atomic_write(SESSIONS, json.dumps(data, indent=1))
        return cwd


def plugin_server_names(home: Path | None = None) -> list[str] | None:
    """The names of the MCP servers of the installed Codex plugins (`plugins/cache/<market>/<plugin>/<version>`): they
    share the tool names of user entries. Every place Codex reads (codex-rs 0.162.0: the manifests `.codex-plugin/`,
    `.claude-plugin/`, `.cursor-plugin/plugin.json` and a root `plugin.json`; `mcpServers` inline or as a path; the
    files `.mcp.json` and `mcp.json`) is read, more is no harm. None when one cannot be read (then no tool name is
    trusted; Gate A and Gate B review, round 2)."""
    base = (home or codex_home()) / "plugins" / "cache"
    names: list[str] = []
    try:
        versions = [v for m in base.iterdir() if m.is_dir() for p in m.iterdir() if p.is_dir()
                    for v in p.iterdir() if v.is_dir()]
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return names
    except OSError:
        return None

    def read(f: Path):
        try:
            return json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError, RecursionError):
            raise LookupError(str(f)) from None

    def servers_of(data: object, mcp_file: bool) -> dict:
        servers = data.get("mcpServers") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
        if servers is None and mcp_file and isinstance(data, dict):
            servers = {k: s for k, s in data.items() if isinstance(s, dict)}
        return servers if isinstance(servers, dict) else {}

    try:
        for v in versions:
            root = os.path.realpath(v)
            files = [v / ".mcp.json", v / "mcp.json"]
            for manifest in (v / ".codex-plugin" / "plugin.json", v / ".claude-plugin" / "plugin.json",
                             v / ".cursor-plugin" / "plugin.json", v / "plugin.json"):
                if not manifest.exists():
                    continue
                data = read(manifest)
                ref = data.get("mcpServers") if isinstance(data, dict) else None
                if isinstance(ref, str):
                    target = os.path.realpath(os.path.join(v, ref))
                    if not target.startswith(root + os.sep):
                        return None
                    files.append(Path(target))
                names.extend(k for k in servers_of(data, False) if isinstance(k, str))
            for f in files:
                if f.exists():
                    names.extend(k for k in servers_of(read(f), True) if isinstance(k, str))
    except LookupError:
        return None
    return names


# ------------------------------------------------------------- tool names --
def _sanitize(s: str) -> str:
    """codex-mcp/src/mcp/mod.rs sanitize_responses_api_tool_name: each character outside [A-Za-z0-9_] becomes `_`."""
    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9_]", "_", s) or "_"


def namespace(server: str) -> str:
    """The tool name prefix of a server, without its `__` delimiter: `mcp__` + sanitized name, trailing `_` trimmed
    (core/src/tools/handlers/mcp.rs join_tool_name). A hashed or shortened name never matches: no note."""
    ns = _sanitize(server)
    ns = ns if ns.startswith("mcp__") else "mcp__" + ns
    return ns.rstrip("_")


def server_of_tool(tool: object, servers, others=()) -> str | None:
    """The one entry of `servers` whose prefix the tool name has, when no name of `others` (every other server Codex
    knows: stdio and switched-off entries, plugin servers) has it too, and the tool part has no `__`: `a` with the
    tool `b__c` and a server `a__b` with the tool `c` make the same name (Gate A and Gate B review)."""
    if not isinstance(tool, str):
        return None
    # a plugin server with the name of a user entry is a second server of that name: it counts
    hits = [n for n in [*servers, *others]
            if tool.startswith(namespace(n) + "__") and len(tool) > len(namespace(n)) + 2]
    if len(hits) != 1 or hits[0] not in servers or "__" in tool[len(namespace(hits[0])) + 2:]:
        return None
    return hits[0]


def mcp_server_of(payload: dict) -> dict | None:
    """The `mcp_server` field that Claude Code sends and Codex does not: {"name", "source": "user"} for a tool of a
    user entry that nothing else can name in this session folder, else None (the token stays text)."""
    if not available():
        return None
    data = codex_helper.load(profile())
    servers = user_servers(data)
    plugins = plugin_server_names()
    if plugins is None:
        return None
    every = data.get("mcp_servers") if isinstance(data, dict) and isinstance(data.get("mcp_servers"), dict) else {}
    name = server_of_tool(payload.get("tool_name"), servers, [*(n for n in every if n not in servers), *plugins])
    if name is None:
        return None
    home = str(codex_home())
    cwd, session = payload.get("cwd"), payload.get("session_id")
    if not isinstance(cwd, str) or not isinstance(session, str) or not session:
        return None
    if _session_folder(session) != cwd:
        return None                          # the session began in another folder, whose layers Codex still uses
    if not codex_helper.user_entry_is_plain(data, name, servers[name]) \
            or codex_helper.profile_files_bring_servers(home) \
            or codex_helper.project_brings_servers(cwd, home):
        return None
    return {"name": name, "source": "user"}


# ------------------------------------------------------------ the helper --
def helper_command(name: str, url: str) -> str:
    """The command line of the entry. `-I`: no environment and no script folder on the import path. The `|| echo`
    keeps the server working when the Python is gone (a helper that fails stops the connection, measured)."""
    import shlex
    parts = [sys.executable, "-I", str(HELPER_FILE), str(STATE), str(codex_home()), name, url]
    return " ".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in parts) + _TAIL


_TAIL = " 2>/dev/null || echo '{}'"


def is_ours(value: object) -> bool:
    """A helper line this maisecrets home wrote: exactly our command, with any Python. A command that only names our
    files (a wrapper of the person's) is not ours (Gate A review)."""
    import shlex
    if not isinstance(value, str) or not value.endswith(_TAIL):
        return False
    try:
        parts = shlex.split(value[:-len(_TAIL)])
    except ValueError:
        return False
    return len(parts) == 7 and parts[1:4] == ["-I", str(HELPER_FILE), str(STATE)] \
        and os.path.realpath(parts[4]) == os.path.realpath(str(codex_home())) \
        and " ".join(shlex.quote(p) for p in parts) + _TAIL == value


def place_helper() -> bool:
    """Copy codex_helper.py to HELPER_FILE when it is missing or differs. Never raises."""
    import shutil
    try:
        src = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "codex_helper.py"
        if HELPER_FILE.exists() and HELPER_FILE.read_bytes() == src.read_bytes():
            return False
        HELPER_FILE.parent.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        tmp = HELPER_FILE.with_name(HELPER_FILE.name + ".tmp")
        shutil.copyfile(src, tmp)
        os.chmod(tmp, 0o600)
        os.replace(tmp, HELPER_FILE)
        return True
    except OSError:
        return False


def write_state(entries: dict[str, tuple[str, str]]) -> None:
    """name -> (url, header) of this profile, read by the helper; an empty dict takes every header back at once."""
    with _lock_for(STATE.parent / ".lock"):
        try:
            data = json.loads(STATE.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            data = {}
        data = data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}
        mine = {n: {"url": u, "header": h} for n, (u, h) in entries.items()}
        if data.get(profile()) == mine or (not mine and profile() not in data):
            return
        if mine:
            data[profile()] = mine
        else:
            data.pop(profile(), None)
        atomic_write(STATE, json.dumps(data, indent=1))


# --------------------------------------------------------- the line edit --
def toml_string(s: str) -> str:
    """A TOML basic string."""
    out = []
    for ch in s:
        if ch in ('"', "\\"):
            out.append("\\" + ch)
        elif ord(ch) < 0x20 or ord(ch) == 0x7F:
            out.append(f"\\u{ord(ch):04X}")
        else:
            out.append(ch)
    return '"' + "".join(out) + '"'


def _header_re(name: str) -> re.Pattern:
    """The table header `[mcp_servers.<name>]` in its bare, basic-quoted or literal-quoted key form."""
    forms = [re.escape(toml_string(name)), re.escape(f"'{name}'")]
    if re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]+", name):
        forms.append(re.escape(name))
    return re.compile(r"[ \t]*\[[ \t]*mcp_servers[ \t]*\.[ \t]*(?:" + "|".join(forms) + r")[ \t]*\][ \t]*(?:#.*)?")


def _expected(data: dict, name: str, value: str | None) -> dict:
    import copy
    want = copy.deepcopy(data)
    spec = want["mcp_servers"][name]
    if value is None:
        spec.pop(HELPER_KEY, None)
    else:
        spec[HELPER_KEY] = value
    return want


def _parse(text: str) -> dict | None:
    toml = codex_helper._toml()
    try:
        data = toml.loads(text) if toml else None
    except (ValueError, RecursionError):
        return None
    return data if isinstance(data, dict) else None


def plan(text: str, name: str, value: str | None) -> str | None:
    """text with the helper line of entry `name` set to value (None: our line removed), or None when that cannot be
    done as one line: another form of the table, two headers, a parse that differs in anything else."""
    data = _parse(text)
    if data is None or not isinstance(data.get("mcp_servers"), dict) or not isinstance(data["mcp_servers"].get(name),
                                                                                         dict):
        return None
    lines = text.splitlines(keepends=True)
    eol = "\r\n" if lines and lines[0].endswith("\r\n") else "\n"
    rx = _header_re(name)
    heads = [i for i, ln in enumerate(lines) if rx.fullmatch(ln.rstrip("\r\n"))]
    if len(heads) != 1:
        return None
    head = heads[0]
    current = data["mcp_servers"][name].get(HELPER_KEY)
    out = list(lines)
    if current is not None:
        if not is_ours(current):
            return None                    # a helper someone else wrote stays
        # our line: the first `http_headers_helper = "…"` line after the table header that decodes to the value
        mine = None
        for i in range(head + 1, len(lines)):
            m = re.fullmatch(r"[ \t]*http_headers_helper[ \t]*=[ \t]*(\".*\")[ \t]*", lines[i].rstrip("\r\n"))
            if m and (_parse("v = " + m.group(1)) or {}).get("v") == current:
                mine = i
                break
        if mine is None:
            return None
        del out[mine]
    if value is not None:
        if not lines[head].endswith(("\n", "\r\n")):
            out[head] = lines[head] + eol
        out.insert(head + 1, f"{HELPER_KEY} = {toml_string(value)}{eol}")
    new = "".join(out)
    return new if _parse(new) == _expected(data, name, value) else None


class _Lock:
    """maisecrets' own lock on the file (Codex takes none): `<file>.maisecrets.lock`, stale after 10 s."""

    def __init__(self, path: Path, wait: float) -> None:
        self.dir, self.wait = path.with_name(path.name + ".maisecrets.lock"), wait

    def __enter__(self):
        deadline = time.time() + self.wait
        while True:
            try:
                os.mkdir(self.dir, 0o700)
                self.ino = os.stat(self.dir).st_ino
                return self
            except FileExistsError:
                try:
                    if time.time() - os.stat(self.dir).st_mtime > LOCK_STALE:
                        os.rmdir(self.dir)
                        continue
                except OSError:
                    continue
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    raise TimeoutError("the config lock is busy") from None
                time.sleep(0.05)

    def __exit__(self, *exc) -> None:
        try:
            if os.stat(self.dir).st_ino == self.ino:
                os.rmdir(self.dir)
        except OSError:
            pass


def in_a_repository(path: str) -> bool:
    """The file, as named or as its link target, lies in a git work tree (a dotfiles repository, a CODEX_HOME inside a
    project): a repository carries it, so maisecrets never writes it (Gate A review: a link in a repository)."""
    return _in_a_repository(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(path))) or \
        _in_a_repository(os.path.dirname(os.path.realpath(path)))


def _in_a_repository(d: str) -> bool:
    while True:
        if os.path.lexists(os.path.join(d, ".git")):
            return True
        parent = os.path.dirname(d)
        if parent == d:
            return False
        d = parent


def may_change_config() -> bool:
    """As vault_refs._may_change_config for Claude Code: a test home never changes the person's Codex config."""
    if not available():
        return False
    if os.environ.get("MAISECRETS_TEST_HOME_OWNED"):
        import tempfile
        return profile().startswith(os.path.realpath(tempfile.gettempdir()) + os.sep)
    return HOME == _DEFAULT_HOME or bool(os.environ.get("CODEX_HOME"))


def _prepare(real: str, text: str) -> str:
    """The new text in a temporary file in the same folder, with the mode of the file, flushed to disk. The caller
    renames it over the file after its last check, so the window in which a write of Codex can be lost is the time of
    one stat and one rename (Codex takes no lock; Gate A and Gate B review)."""
    try:
        mode = os.stat(real).st_mode & 0o777
    except OSError:
        mode = 0o600
    tmp = f"{real}.maisecrets.{os.getpid()}.tmp"
    try:
        os.unlink(tmp)
    except OSError:
        pass
    fd = os.open(tmp, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0), mode)
    try:
        with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
            f.write(text)
            f.flush()
            os.fsync(f.fileno())
    except BaseException:
        _drop(tmp)
        raise
    return tmp


def _drop(path: str) -> None:
    try:
        os.unlink(path)
    except OSError:
        pass


def _same(path: Path, raw: bytes, st: os.stat_result) -> bool:
    try:
        now = os.stat(path)
        return (now.st_size, now.st_mtime_ns, now.st_ino) == (st.st_size, st.st_mtime_ns, st.st_ino) \
            and path.read_bytes() == raw
    except OSError:
        return False


BACKUP_DIR = HOME / "vault-refs"     # the backups lie in maisecrets' own folder: no file of the person goes


def backup_path() -> Path:
    """One backup per Codex profile (Gate B review, round 2)."""
    import hashlib
    return BACKUP_DIR / f"codex-config-{hashlib.sha256(profile().encode()).hexdigest()[:12]}.toml.backup"


def _backup(data: bytes) -> str:
    backup = str(backup_path())
    BACKUP_DIR.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    try:
        os.unlink(backup)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        pass
    fd = os.open(backup, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0), 0o600)
    with os.fdopen(fd, "wb") as f:
        f.write(data)
    return backup


def apply_config(wanted: dict[str, tuple[str, str]], lock_wait: float = 5.0) -> tuple[list[str], list[str], Path,
                                                                                         Path]:
    """The helper lines of the user config as they should be: ours on each wanted entry (name -> (url, header)) whose
    URL is still that URL, none of ours elsewhere. The state file first, so a header goes at once when it is taken
    back. Returns (added, removed, path, backup). A symlinked config is written through to its target, as Codex does."""
    path = Path(profile())
    backup = backup_path()
    write_state(wanted)
    if not path.is_file() or in_a_repository(str(config_path())):
        return [], [], path, backup
    for _attempt in range(2):
        try:
            st = os.stat(path)
            raw = path.read_bytes()
            text = raw.decode("utf-8")
        except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError):
            return [], [], path, backup
        data = _parse(text)
        if data is None:
            return [], [], path, backup       # a file that does not parse is not touched
        new, added, removed = text, [], []
        plain = user_servers(data)
        servers = data.get("mcp_servers") if isinstance(data.get("mcp_servers"), dict) else {}
        for name, spec in servers.items():
            if not isinstance(name, str) or not isinstance(spec, dict):
                continue
            url = plain.get(name)
            have = spec.get(HELPER_KEY)
            want = helper_command(name, url) if url and wanted.get(name, ("",))[0] == url else None
            if want is not None and any(isinstance(spec.get(key), dict) and any(
                    isinstance(k, str) and k.lower() == codex_helper.HEADER.lower() for k in spec[key])
                    for key in ("http_headers", "env_http_headers")):
                continue                       # a Client-Vault header someone else wrote (also from the environment)
            if have == want or (want is None and not is_ours(have)):
                continue
            step = plan(new, name, want)
            if step is None:
                continue                       # another form of the table: left as it is
            new = step
            (added if want is not None else removed).append(name)
        if new == text:
            try:
                backup.unlink()                # nothing to change: the backup of the last change goes
            except OSError:
                pass
            return [], [], path, backup
        with _Lock(path, lock_wait):
            if not _same(path, raw, st):
                continue                       # Codex wrote the file meanwhile: once more from its new text
            _backup(raw)
            tmp = _prepare(str(path), new)
            if not _same(path, raw, st):
                _drop(tmp)
                continue
            try:
                os.replace(tmp, str(path))
            finally:
                _drop(tmp)
        return added, removed, path, backup
    return [], [], path, backup


def notice(name: str) -> str:
    from .vault_refs import HEADER, _say_name
    return (f"maisecrets: the MCP server {_say_name(name)} supports {HEADER}. From the next start of Codex, "
            "maisecrets puts this server's values into your local files and commands; the AI still sees only "
            "placeholders. To turn it off, send this alone as your prompt: maisecrets: set client_header off. More: "
            "README, section "
            "\"Vault references\".")

"""`maisecrets consent-report ssh`: what the ssh consent check (C21) would ask for the Bash commands of earlier
Claude Code sessions on this computer, so that a person can check the check on their own work before or after
turning it on (#15: the rules were tuned on one person's sessions).

It reads the local transcripts, classifies each Bash command that names an ssh-family word with the check of this
version, and prints counts per verdict and per reason. A reason names no host, no command and no value. It runs
nothing, changes no setting or consent, and sends nothing. With --excerpts FILE it writes a short window around
each question into a new file of the person's own, for a look by hand: the values the detectors find are masked,
hosts and commands stay in it, and the person decides what to share.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import collections
import json
import os
import re
import time
from pathlib import Path

from . import detect, hooks, ssh_consent

USAGE = "maisecrets consent-report ssh [--last 30d | --last N] [--json] [--excerpts FILE]"


def _parse(text: str):
    ctxs = hooks._shell_contexts(text)
    return hooks._segments(text, ctxs), ctxs


def _sessions(base: Path, last: str) -> list[Path]:
    """The transcripts of the last N days (30d) or the N newest ones (200), oldest first."""
    found = []
    for p in base.glob("*/*.jsonl"):
        try:
            found.append((p.stat().st_mtime, p))
        except OSError:
            continue                         # a dangling link or a file that went away
    found.sort()
    if last.endswith("d"):
        cutoff = time.time() - int(last[:-1]) * 86400
        return [p for t, p in found if t >= cutoff]
    return [p for _t, p in found[-int(last):]]


def _commands(path: Path) -> list[str]:
    out = []
    try:
        with open(path, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as f:
            for line in f:
                if '"Bash"' not in line:
                    continue
                try:
                    rec = json.loads(line)
                except ValueError:
                    continue
                msg = rec.get("message") if isinstance(rec, dict) else None
                content = msg.get("content") if isinstance(msg, dict) else None
                for block in content if isinstance(content, list) else []:
                    if isinstance(block, dict) and block.get("type") == "tool_use" and block.get("name") == "Bash":
                        tool_input = block.get("input")
                        cmd = tool_input.get("command", "") if isinstance(tool_input, dict) else ""
                        if isinstance(cmd, str) and ssh_consent._TOKEN_RE.search(cmd):
                            out.append(cmd)
    except OSError:
        pass
    return out


_VOCABULARY: set = set()
# the slot of a reason that names the program the check saw: `argument of X`, `started by X`, `URL for X`, …
_PROGRAM_SLOT = re.compile(r"(argument of|started by|URL for|piped into|pipes text into|code that) ([^\s,]+)|"
                           r"(in a) ([^\s,]+)(?= command that runs)")


def _known_programs() -> set:
    return (ssh_consent._LAUNCHERS | ssh_consent._SHELLS | ssh_consent._URL_CLIENTS | ssh_consent._CONTAINER_EXEC
            | ssh_consent._RUN_TEXT | {"gh", "glab", "git", "find", "xargs", "parallel", "crontab", "at", "batch"})


def _reason(why: str) -> str:
    """The reason with no host, command or value of the person's: the program slot keeps a name only from a closed
    list of program names (./acme-billing-prod, root@web1:~# are the person's); a quoted part goes; and, as a second
    layer, a word that holds a host character (@ : ~ / #) or that the check's own source does not hold goes too
    (codex and Opus, review of #15)."""
    if not _VOCABULARY:
        _VOCABULARY.update(w.lower() for w in re.findall(r"[A-Za-z][\w.-]*", Path(ssh_consent.__file__).read_text(
            encoding="utf-8")))
    known = _known_programs()
    def slot(m: re.Match) -> str:
        verb, name = (m.group(1), m.group(2)) if m.group(1) else (m.group(3), m.group(4))
        return f"{verb} {name if name.lower() in known else '…'}"
    text = _PROGRAM_SLOT.sub(slot, why or "")
    text = re.sub(r"'[^']*'|\"[^\"]*\"", "'…'", text)
    def word(w: re.Match) -> str:
        x = w.group(0)
        kept = x in ("~/.ssh", "~/.ssh,") or x.isdigit() or re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z][A-Za-z-]*\.?", x) \
            and x.rstrip(".").lower() in _VOCABULARY
        return x if kept else "…"
    return re.sub(r"[^\s,'…]+", word, text)


def _excerpt(cmd: str, why: str) -> str:
    """A short window around the word the verdict names, with every value the detectors find masked."""
    word = re.search(r"'([^']*)'", why or "")
    m = re.search(re.escape(word.group(1)), cmd) if word else ssh_consent._TOKEN_RE.search(cmd)
    lo, hi = (max(0, m.start() - 60), min(len(cmd), m.end() + 60)) if m else (0, 120)
    # the whole command is scanned first: a window that cuts a value would hide it from the detectors (codex, round 5)
    spans = []
    for hit in detect.scan(cmd):
        at = cmd.find(hit.value)
        while at >= 0:
            spans.append((at, at + len(hit.value)))
            at = cmd.find(hit.value, at + 1)
    out, k = [], lo
    for a, b in sorted(spans):
        if b <= k or a >= hi:
            continue
        out.append(cmd[k:max(a, k)])
        out.append("<value>")
        k = max(k, b)
    out.append(cmd[k:hi] if k < hi else "")
    return "".join(out).replace("\n", "\\n")


def _write_new(path: str, lines: list[str]) -> None:
    """A new file for the person only: no symlink followed, no file of another name replaced."""
    fd = os.open(path, os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0), 0o600)
    with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.writelines(line + "\n" for line in lines)


def run(args: list[str]) -> int:
    if not args or args[0] != "ssh":
        print(USAGE)
        return 2
    args = args[1:]
    last, as_json, excerpts = "30d", False, ""
    while args:
        a = args.pop(0)
        if a == "--json":
            as_json = True
        elif a == "--last" and args and re.fullmatch(r"[1-9]\d*d?", args[0]):
            last = args.pop(0)
        elif a == "--excerpts" and args:
            excerpts = args.pop(0)
        else:
            print(USAGE)
            return 2
    base = Path(os.environ.get("CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR") or Path.home() / ".claude") / "projects"
    kinds: collections.Counter = collections.Counter()
    reasons: collections.Counter = collections.Counter()
    sessions = commands = write_hosts = 0
    windows: list[str] = []
    for path in _sessions(base, last) if base.is_dir() else []:
        cmds = _commands(path)
        if not cmds:
            continue
        sessions += 1
        seen: set[str] = set()
        for cmd in cmds:
            commands += 1
            v = ssh_consent.classify(cmd, _parse)
            kinds[v.kind] += 1
            if v.kind == "write":
                new = set(v.hosts) - seen
                write_hosts += len(new)      # without a consent, a write asks once per host and session
                seen |= set(v.hosts)
                if excerpts and new:         # a window for each question, the writes too (codex, round 7)
                    windows.append(json.dumps({"kind": v.kind, "why": _reason(v.why), "excerpt": _excerpt(cmd, v.why)},
                                              ensure_ascii=False))
            elif v.kind in ("unknown", "deny"):
                reasons[(v.kind, _reason(v.why))] += 1
                if excerpts:
                    windows.append(json.dumps({"kind": v.kind, "why": _reason(v.why), "excerpt": _excerpt(cmd, v.why)},
                                              ensure_ascii=False))
    if excerpts:
        try:
            _write_new(excerpts, windows)
        except OSError as exc:
            print(f"maisecrets consent-report: cannot create the file you named ({exc.strerror}). Name a new file.")
            return 1
    result = {"sessions": sessions, "commands": commands, "kinds": dict(kinds), "write_hosts": write_hosts,
              "questions": [{"kind": k, "why": w, "n": n} for (k, w), n in reasons.most_common()], "last": last}
    if as_json:
        print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
        return 0
    print(f"ssh consent report, sessions of the last {last if last.endswith('d') else last + ' sessions'} "
          "(the Claude Code transcripts on this computer):")
    print(f"  {sessions} sessions, {commands} Bash commands that name an ssh-family word")
    print(f"  none {kinds['none']}, read {kinds['read']} (no question), write {kinds['write']} "
          f"({write_hosts} questions without a consent: one per host and session), "
          f"unknown {kinds['unknown']} (a question each), refused {kinds['deny']}")
    if reasons:
        print("  questions and refusals per reason:")
        for (k, w), n in reasons.most_common():
            print(f"    {n:5}  {k:7}  {w}")
    if excerpts:
        print(f"  {len(windows)} windows in the file you named, for a look by hand; share only what you may share.")
    print("  Nothing ran and nothing was sent; Codex sessions are not read.")
    return 0

"""The consents of `ssh_consent` (maisecrets/ssh_consent.py): which hosts a write may reach without a question.

A consent covers a host, or the hosts of its group in `ssh_host_groups`, for one session and one agent
(the main thread or one subagent: subagents share the session id, and a subagent that read untrusted
text must not inherit the main thread's yes; plan review of #8, 2026-10-06), for APPROVAL_HOURS.

How a yes is proven, since the hook cannot see the answer to its own `ask` and a hook entry point can be
called by anyone (an agent can run `run.sh post-tool` with a payload it wrote):

- Claude Code: the native yes to the ask allows that one command and records nothing (a yes that silently
  opened an 8-hour window surprised the person, 2026-10-07). A window opens only from the person's own prompt
  `maisecrets: allow ssh HOST` (source user, the whole prompt; a tool call that carries it is refused).
- Codex, which cannot ask: the refusal names a sentence with a code that the hook made at that moment
  (`maisecrets: allow ssh HOST CODE`); typed alone as the next prompt, it grants. Text written before the
  refusal (a pasted document, a headless prompt built from an issue) cannot know the code.

The store keeps only hashes of tokens and codes. Limit, as for every file of the user: a program that runs
as the user outside the sandbox can write this store; the hook refuses commands that name it.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import secrets
import time

from .vault import HOME, _lock_for, atomic_write, read_text_retry

APPROVAL_SECONDS = 8 * 3600
CODE_SECONDS = 10 * 60
STORE = "ssh-consent.json"


def _path():
    return HOME / STORE


def _h(s: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(s.encode()).hexdigest()


def _who(session: str, agent: str | None) -> str:
    return f"{session}|{agent or ''}"


def _load() -> dict:
    try:
        data = json.loads(read_text_retry(_path()))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        data = {}
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        data = {}
    for k in ("codes", "approved"):
        if not isinstance(data.get(k), dict):
            data[k] = {}
    now = time.time()
    data["codes"] = {k: v for k, v in data["codes"].items() if isinstance(v, dict) and v.get("until", 0) > now}
    for who, hosts in list(data["approved"].items()):
        kept = {h: u for h, u in (hosts.items() if isinstance(hosts, dict) else []) if isinstance(u, (int, float))
                and u > now}
        if kept:
            data["approved"][who] = kept
        else:
            del data["approved"][who]
    return data


def _save(data: dict) -> None:
    atomic_write(_path(), json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True))


def covered(session: str | None, agent: str | None, hosts: list[str]) -> bool:
    """Whether every host has an unexpired consent for this session and agent."""
    if not session or not hosts:
        return False
    approved = _load()["approved"].get(_who(session, agent), {})
    return all(h in approved for h in hosts)


def grant_typed(session: str, hosts: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    """The person typed `maisecrets: allow ssh HOST` as their own prompt (Claude Code, source user): writes to these
    hosts run without a question for APPROVAL_SECONDS, in this session, for the main thread. A subagent asks for
    itself, as before."""
    with _lock_for(HOME / ".ssh-consent.lock"):
        data = _load()
        until = time.time() + APPROVAL_SECONDS
        data["approved"].setdefault(_who(session, None), {}).update({h: until for h in hosts})
        _save(data)
    return sorted(hosts)


def new_code(session: str, agent: str | None, hosts: list[str]) -> str:
    """A code for the Codex sentence, made when the hook refuses; six digits, ten minutes, one use."""
    code = f"{secrets.randbelow(10 ** 6):06d}"
    with _lock_for(HOME / ".ssh-consent.lock"):
        data = _load()
        data["codes"][_h(f"{session}|{code}")] = {"who": _who(session, agent), "hosts": sorted(set(hosts)),
                                                  "until": time.time() + CODE_SECONDS}
        _save(data)
    return code


def grant_by_code(session: str, host: str, code: str) -> list[str] | None:
    """The person typed the sentence with its host and code: the hosts it grants, or None."""
    with _lock_for(HOME / ".ssh-consent.lock"):
        data = _load()
        rec = data["codes"].get(_h(f"{session}|{code}"))
        if not rec or host not in rec["hosts"]:
            return None
        del data["codes"][_h(f"{session}|{code}")]
        until = time.time() + APPROVAL_SECONDS
        data["approved"].setdefault(rec["who"], {}).update({h: until for h in rec["hosts"]})
        _save(data)
    return rec["hosts"]


def drop_codes(session: str) -> None:
    """A prompt that is not the sentence ends the open codes of its session: the refusal says "the next prompt"."""
    data = _load()
    if not any(str(v.get("who", "")).startswith(session + "|") for v in data["codes"].values()):
        return
    with _lock_for(HOME / ".ssh-consent.lock"):
        data = _load()
        data["codes"] = {k: v for k, v in data["codes"].items() if not str(v.get("who", "")).startswith(session + "|")}
        _save(data)

"""The debug log of the hooks (hooks.py imports _debug again, so hooks._debug is this function)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os


def _debug(msg: str) -> None:
    """Append a line to $MAISECRETS_DEBUG_LOG when set (harness and troubleshooting); never a value."""
    log = os.environ.get("MAISECRETS_DEBUG_LOG")
    if log:
        try:
            with open(log, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(msg + "\n")
        except OSError:
            pass

"""Where each stored secret was sent (setting `secret_destinations`: observe by default, or off).
Mcpgate-de/maisecrets#13.

Observe only: this module never decides whether a call runs. It notes, on this computer only, each destination a
stored secret went to, and shows it in /maisecrets:list. A seen destination is a record, never a permission: `seen`
and `allowed` are kept apart in the store, and `allowed` is not used by any decision yet (a later protect mode).

Destinations:
- network: a host from a URL in a Bash command, an ssh host (as maisecrets/ssh_consent.py reads it), an MCP server
  and tool. The tool, not the server, because one gateway server carries many services, one tool each; an `action`
  is the operation inside a service, not a destination (scripts/measure_binding.py, 2026-10-07: with `action` in the
  key 16 of 56 secrets would have several destinations, with server and tool 5). A tool that serves several services
  names its service in a `service` or `provider` field, which then joins the key.
- local: a file (Write, Edit) or a Bash command without a host. Listed, never a destination, never a hint.
A label is cleaned (one printable line, a secret shape hidden, a service field only as a name), and the record of a
secret keeps its MAX_PER_SECRET most recent destinations.

The one hint (maisecrets/settings.py HINTS) comes at the first pattern break: a secret with ESTABLISHED_USES uses on
one day at one network destination goes to a network destination it never went to. 3 uses on one day was measured
as the threshold (scripts/measure_binding.py over 90 days of one user's transcripts: 1/1 gave 56 breaks, 2/1 18,
3/1 8, 3/2 1, 5/2 0); a stricter one would almost never fire, a looser one counts the setup of a new token.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import math
import os
import re
import time
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import urlparse

from .vault import HOME, LockTimeout, _Lock, atomic_write

ESTABLISHED_USES = 3
STORE = "destinations.json"
class _ShortLock(_Lock):
    """The vault lock waits up to 6 s, which is right for the store and wrong here: a busy record must cost a call
    at most this long, and then the record is skipped (codex review of 0.6.7: two keys waited 12 s)."""
    LOCK_DEADLINE = 0.3


_LOCK = _ShortLock(HOME / ".destinations.lock")
_URL = re.compile(r"https?://[^\s'\"<>`|;)]+")
_MAX_SESSIONS = 50


def _path() -> Path:
    return HOME / STORE


class Corrupt(ValueError):
    pass


_FIELDS = {"kind": str, "label": str, "uses": int, "first": (int, float), "last": (int, float), "day": str,
           "day_uses": int, "max_day_uses": int}
MAX_PER_SECRET = 50       # a call the model writes chooses a label; the record of one secret stays bounded
# a call is noted as pending when the value is handed out (PreToolUse) and becomes `seen` only when the client
# reports that it ran (PostToolUse or PostToolUseFailure): a call the person declines leaves no record (ChatGPT
# review of 0.6.7). A pending call that never ran goes after this time; at most this many wait at once. The time
# counts from the hand-out, before the permission dialog, so it is long: a dialog left open is still a call to come
PENDING_SECONDS = 24 * 3600
MAX_PENDING = 100


def _call_key(call: str, session: str | None, agent: str | None) -> str:
    """A tool_use_id is unique only in its session (Codex numbers them: call_1): the end of a call in another
    session or agent must not commit this one (codex review of 0.6.8)."""
    return "\x1f".join((session or "", agent or "", call))


def _well_formed(d) -> bool:
    return isinstance(d, dict) and all(isinstance(d.get(k), t) and not isinstance(d.get(k), bool)
                                       and not (isinstance(d.get(k), float) and not math.isfinite(d[k]))
                                       for k, t in _FIELDS.items())


def _clean(data: dict) -> dict:
    """Keep only well-formed records: a file another program changed must not crash the list (codex review)."""
    secrets = data.get("secrets") if isinstance(data.get("secrets"), dict) else {}
    kept = {}
    for key, rec in secrets.items():
        if not (isinstance(key, str) and isinstance(rec, dict) and isinstance(rec.get("seen", {}), dict)):
            continue
        seen = {i: d for i, d in rec.get("seen", {}).items() if isinstance(i, str) and _well_formed(d)}
        if len(seen) > MAX_PER_SECRET:
            seen = dict(sorted(seen.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]["last"])[-MAX_PER_SECRET:])
        kept[key] = {"seen": seen, "allowed": rec.get("allowed") if isinstance(rec.get("allowed"), dict) else {}}
    data["secrets"] = kept
    calls = data.get("pending_calls") if isinstance(data.get("pending_calls"), dict) else {}
    data["pending_calls"] = {i: c for i, c in calls.items() if isinstance(i, str) and _pending_well_formed(c)}
    return data


def _pending_well_formed(c) -> bool:
    return (isinstance(c, dict) and isinstance(c.get("keys"), list) and all(isinstance(k, str) for k in c["keys"])
            and isinstance(c.get("found"), list) and len(c["found"]) <= MAX_PER_SECRET
            and all(isinstance(f, list) and len(f) == 2 and f[0] in ("network", "local") and isinstance(f[1], str)
                    for f in c["found"])
            and all(c.get(k) is None or isinstance(c.get(k), str) for k in ("session", "agent"))
            and isinstance(c.get("t"), (int, float)) and not isinstance(c.get("t"), bool) and math.isfinite(c["t"]))


def _load(strict: bool = False) -> dict:
    """The store, cleaned. With strict, a file that exists and does not parse raises Corrupt: a writer must not
    replace what it cannot read (it moves it aside first, see _load_for_write)."""
    try:
        data = json.loads(_path().read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except FileNotFoundError:
        data = {}
    except (OSError, ValueError, RecursionError):
        if strict:
            raise Corrupt(STORE)
        data = {}
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        if strict:
            raise Corrupt(STORE)
        data = {}
    data = _clean(data)
    for k in ("secrets", "pending_hint", "interactive", "pending_calls"):
        if not isinstance(data.get(k), dict if k != "interactive" else list):
            data[k] = {} if k != "interactive" else []
    return data


def _save(data: dict) -> None:
    atomic_write(_path(), json.dumps(data, indent=1, sort_keys=True) + "\n")


def _load_for_write() -> dict:
    """Under the lock: the store to change. A corrupt file is moved aside (destinations.json.corrupt) and a new one
    begins, so nothing the person had is overwritten unseen; /maisecrets:status lists the file."""
    try:
        return _load(strict=True)
    except Corrupt:
        aside = _path().with_name(STORE + ".corrupt")
        n = 0
        while aside.exists():
            n += 1
            aside = _path().with_name(f"{STORE}.corrupt.{int(time.time())}.{n}")
        os.replace(_path(), aside)   # an OSError ends this note: a file not moved aside is never written over
        return _load()


def hosts_in(command: str) -> list[str]:
    hosts = set()
    for url in _URL.findall(command or ""):
        try:
            host = urlparse(url).hostname
        except ValueError:
            continue
        if host:
            hosts.add(host.lower())
    return sorted(hosts)


_UNPRINTABLE = re.compile(r"[\x00-\x1f\x7f-\x9f\u200b-\u200f\u202a-\u202e\u2066-\u2069\ufeff]")


def clean_label(text: str) -> str:
    """One printable line of at most 80 characters: a label comes from a call the model wrote (a service field, a
    path), and a newline or a terminal escape in it must not draw lines into /maisecrets:list (codex review)."""
    text = _UNPRINTABLE.sub("?", str(text))
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
    text = "".join(c if c.isprintable() else "?" for c in text)   # format characters too: U+2060, U+00AD (codex)
    from . import detect
    for m in sorted(detect.scan(text), key=lambda m: -m.start):
        if m.type == "SECRET":
            text = text[:m.start] + "<hidden>" + text[m.end:]
    return text[:77] + "..." if len(text) > 80 else text


def destinations_of(tool: str, tool_input: dict, ssh_hosts: list[str] | None = None) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """(kind, label) per destination of one call: kind "network" or "local". The label is what the person reads."""
    if tool in ("Bash", "PowerShell"):
        command = str(tool_input.get("command") or "")
        if tool == "Bash":
            from .hooks import _shell_contexts, comment_is_sure   # a URL in a # comment goes nowhere (codex review)
            ctxs = _shell_contexts(command)
            command = "".join(" " if cx == "comment" and comment_is_sure(command, ctxs, k) else ch
                              for k, (ch, cx) in enumerate(zip(command, ctxs)))
        # the ssh hosts first: a bounded record keeps the first entries, and many URLs must not push them out (Opus)
        found = [("network", "ssh " + h) for h in sorted(set(ssh_hosts or []))]
        found += [("network", h) for h in hosts_in(command)]
        return [(k, clean_label(v)) for k, v in found] or [("local", "a command on this computer")]
    if tool.startswith("mcp__"):
        parts = tool.split("__")
        server, name = parts[1] if len(parts) > 1 else "?", parts[2] if len(parts) > 2 else "?"
        service = tool_input.get("service") or tool_input.get("provider")
        # a name, not free text: the model writes this field, and a value in it must not land in the record (Opus)
        if not (isinstance(service, str) and re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,40}", service)):
            service = ""
        label = f"MCP {server} · {name}" + (f" · {service}" if service else "")
        return [("network", clean_label(label))]
    if tool in ("Write", "Edit", "MultiEdit", "NotebookEdit", "apply_patch"):
        path = str(tool_input.get("file_path") or tool_input.get("notebook_path") or "")
        folder = os.path.dirname(path) or "."
        home = str(Path.home())
        if folder == home or folder.startswith(home.rstrip(os.sep) + os.sep):    # not /home/anna for /home/ann
            folder = "~" + folder[len(home):]
        if os.sep == "\\":
            folder = folder.replace("\\", "/")         # one separator in a label: ~/proj/, not ~\proj/ (Windows CI)
        return [("local", clean_label(f"a file in {folder}/"))]
    return [("local", clean_label(f"a {tool} call"))]


def note(keys: list[str], session: str | None, agent: str | None, destinations: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> bool:
    """Record one use per key and destination, all keys of a call under one short lock. True when the call is a
    pattern break (the caller gives the hint only once, globally). Never raises: a record that cannot be written
    must not stop the call. For a call the client did not yet report as run, use pend() and commit()."""
    try:
        with _LOCK:
            data = _load_for_write()
            pattern_break = _note_locked(data, keys, session, agent, destinations)
            _save(data)
            return pattern_break
    except (OSError, LockTimeout, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError):
        return False


def _note_locked(data: dict, keys: list[str], session: str | None, agent: str | None,
                 destinations: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> bool:
    pattern_break = False
    for key in dict.fromkeys(keys):
        pattern_break = _note_one(data, key, destinations) or pattern_break
    if pattern_break and session and not agent and session in data["interactive"]:
        data["pending_hint"][session] = time.time()
    data["pending_hint"] = {s: t for s, t in data["pending_hint"].items() if s in data["interactive"]}
    return pattern_break


def pend(call: str, keys: list[str], session: str | None, agent: str | None,
         destinations: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> None:
    """The value is handed out to this call: wait for the client to report that it ran. Nothing is `seen` yet and
    no pattern break is counted. Never raises."""
    try:
        with _LOCK:
            data = _load_for_write()
            now = time.time()
            calls = {i: c for i, c in data["pending_calls"].items() if now - c["t"] < PENDING_SECONDS}
            found = list(destinations)[:MAX_PER_SECRET]     # bounded, as a record (destinations_of names a host once)
            calls[_call_key(call, session, agent)] = {"keys": list(dict.fromkeys(keys)), "session": session,
                                                      "agent": agent, "found": [[k, v] for k, v in found], "t": now}
            if len(calls) > MAX_PENDING:
                calls = dict(sorted(calls.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1]["t"])[-MAX_PENDING:])
            data["pending_calls"] = calls
            _save(data)
    except (OSError, LockTimeout, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
        from . import incidents
        incidents.note("destinations.pend", exc)


def commit(call: str, session: str | None, agent: str | None) -> bool:
    """The client reports that this call ran (also one that failed: the tool had the value). Its pending record
    becomes `seen`, however late the report comes: it is the proof (Opus review of 0.6.8). True when it is a pattern
    break. A call with no pending record changes nothing and takes no lock. Never raises."""
    key = _call_key(call, session, agent)
    try:
        if key not in _load()["pending_calls"]:
            return False                 # most calls carry no value: no lock and no write for them
        with _LOCK:
            data = _load_for_write()
            c = data["pending_calls"].pop(key, None)
            if c is None:
                return False
            pattern_break = _note_locked(data, c["keys"], c["session"], c["agent"],
                                         [(k, v) for k, v in c["found"]])
            _save(data)
            return pattern_break
    except (OSError, LockTimeout, ValueError, TypeError, KeyError) as exc:
        from . import incidents
        incidents.note("destinations.commit", exc)
        return False


def _note_one(data: dict, key: str, destinations: list[tuple[str, str]]) -> bool:
    rec = data["secrets"].setdefault(key, {"seen": {}, "allowed": {}})
    seen = rec.setdefault("seen", {})
    rec.setdefault("allowed", {})          # kept apart from seen; no decision reads it yet
    now = time.time()
    today = time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
    established = any(d.get("kind") == "network" and d.get("max_day_uses", 0) >= ESTABLISHED_USES
                      for d in seen.values())
    pattern_break = False
    for kind, label in destinations:
        ident = f"{kind}:{label}"
        d = seen.get(ident)
        if d is None:
            if kind == "network" and established:
                pattern_break = True
            if len(seen) >= MAX_PER_SECRET:
                del seen[min(seen, key=lambda i: seen[i]["last"])]     # the oldest goes
            d = seen[ident] = {"kind": kind, "label": label, "uses": 0, "first": now, "last": now,
                               "day": today, "day_uses": 0, "max_day_uses": 0}
        if d.get("day") != today:
            d["day"], d["day_uses"] = today, 0
        d["uses"] += 1
        d["day_uses"] += 1
        d["max_day_uses"] = max(d.get("max_day_uses", 0), d["day_uses"])
        d["last"] = now
    return pattern_break


def mark_interactive(session: str) -> None:
    """The person typed a prompt in this session: a hint here has a reader (a headless run has none)."""
    try:
        with _LOCK:
            data = _load_for_write()
            if session in data["interactive"]:
                return
            data["interactive"] = (data["interactive"] + [session])[-_MAX_SESSIONS:]
            _save(data)
    except (OSError, LockTimeout, ValueError, RecursionError):
        pass


def take_pending(session: str | None) -> bool:
    """A pattern break of this session waits for its hint; take it (once)."""
    if not session:
        return False
    try:
        with _LOCK:
            data = _load_for_write()
            if data["pending_hint"].pop(session, None) is None:
                return False
            _save(data)
            return True
    except (OSError, LockTimeout, ValueError):
        return False


def of(key: str) -> dict:
    return _load()["secrets"].get(key, {"seen": {}, "allowed": {}})


def summary() -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """(secrets with a network destination, network destinations, secrets with more than one)."""
    secrets = _load()["secrets"]
    counts = [sum(1 for d in r.get("seen", {}).values() if d.get("kind") == "network") for r in secrets.values()]
    counts = [c for c in counts if c]
    return len(counts), sum(counts), sum(1 for c in counts if c > 1)


NEW_SECONDS = 24 * 3600   # `new` in the list: first seen in the last day. Not "since the list was opened": the
                          # model runs the list too (commands/list.md), and a list it ran would clear the marks (Opus)


def uses_text(n: int) -> str:
    return "1×" if n <= 1 else "2–9×" if n < 10 else "10+×"


def when_text(ts: float, now: float | None = None) -> str:
    age = max(0.0, (now or time.time()) - ts)
    if age < 3600:
        return "less than an hour ago"
    if age < 86400:
        return f"{int(age // 3600)} hours ago" if age >= 7200 else "1 hour ago"
    days = int(age // 86400)
    return "1 day ago" if days == 1 else f"{days} days ago"


def forget(keys: list[str]) -> bool:
    """Drop the records of these keys. False when the record could not be written: the caller says so."""
    try:
        with _LOCK:
            data = _load_for_write()
            for k in keys:
                data["secrets"].pop(k, None)
            gone = set(keys)
            for i, c in list(data["pending_calls"].items()):      # a call that did not yet run names the key too
                c["keys"] = [k for k in c["keys"] if k not in gone]
                if not c["keys"]:
                    del data["pending_calls"][i]
            _save(data)
            return True
    except (OSError, LockTimeout, ValueError):
        return False


def wipe() -> bool:
    """Delete the whole record under its lock (a writer that holds it would write it back after an unlink)."""
    try:
        with _LOCK:
            for p in [_path(), *HOME.glob(STORE + ".corrupt*")]:
                try:
                    p.unlink()
                except FileNotFoundError:
                    pass
            return True
    except (OSError, LockTimeout):
        return False

"""Compatibility: the detector lives in maisecrets/detection/core.py.

This name is the same module object, so an import or a patch through it reaches the core itself."""
import sys

from .detection import core as _module

sys.modules[__name__] = _module

"""The detection core of maisecrets: it finds secrets and personal data in text, and holds no plugin, home folder or
reference form of its own. A caller gives those to a Detector:

    from maisecrets.detection import Active, Detector, Host, NO_REFERENCES, NO_SOURCES
    d = Detector(regions=Active(regions=("generic", "de"), languages=("en", "de"), source="mine"),
                 sources=NO_SOURCES, references=NO_REFERENCES, host=Host(user="me", year=2026))
    hits = d.scan(text, channel="prompt")

Each argument left None takes the plugin's default when the plugin is there (the config of this computer, the
readers of the home folder, the placeholder form, this computer's user name and year). The names below are the API.
The rule tables and the helpers of maisecrets/detection/core.py are not: maisecrets.detect keeps them for the
plugin and its tests, and they change with the rules.
"""
from .core import NO_REFERENCES, NO_SOURCES, THIS_COMPUTER, Detector, Host, Match, References, Rule, Sources, user_rule
from .regions import Active, resolve as resolve_regions
from .vocabulary import TYPES

__all__ = ["Active", "Detector", "Host", "Match", "NO_REFERENCES", "NO_SOURCES", "References", "Rule", "Sources",
           "THIS_COMPUTER", "TYPES", "resolve_regions", "user_rule"]

"""python3 -m maisecrets.detection < request.json: the contract maisecrets.detect/1 (contract.py)."""
import sys

from .contract import main

sys.exit(main())

"""Check digits and checksums for the detector (detect.py): national identifiers that Presidio validates but whose
validators were not yet ported, the identifiers of regions Presidio has no rules for (Switzerland, Austria), the German
document number, and three kinds of crypto wallet secret (a BIP39 seed phrase, a WIF private key, a BIP32 extended
private key).

Each function takes the matched text and returns True only for a value whose checksum holds. A function that cannot
parse its value returns False. No function here reads a file except the BIP39 word list, once.

Sources of the algorithms: Presidio's validate_result of each recognizer (MIT, see rules/LICENSE-presidio) for PESEL,
NIF, NIE, NHS, ABA and the codice fiscale; ICAO Doc 9303 Part 3 for the 7-3-1 check digit; eCH-0097 for the Swiss UID;
the AHV number is an EAN-13; the Austrian numbers follow the published algorithms of the Hauptverband (VSNR) and of the
BMF (UID). The Swiss rules follow a fork of this project (Apache-2.0, see NOTICE). BIP39, BIP32 and the WIF format are
the Bitcoin Improvement Proposals 39 and 32 and the Base58Check encoding.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import binascii
import hashlib
import re
import string
from pathlib import Path

RULES_DIR = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "rules"


def _digits(v: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"\D", "", v)


def _luhn_sum(digits: str) -> int:
    """The Luhn sum of a digit string whose last digit is not doubled (a complete number)."""
    total = 0
    for i, ch in enumerate(reversed(digits)):
        d = int(ch)
        if i % 2:
            d = d * 2 - 9 if d > 4 else d * 2
        total += d
    return total % 10


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ Germany --
# ICAO Doc 9303 Part 3, 4.9: a digit counts its value, A to Z count 10 to 35, the filler < counts 0; weights 7, 3, 1
def icao_check_digit(s: str) -> int:
    total = 0
    for i, c in enumerate(s):
        if c.isdigit():
            val = int(c)
        elif "A" <= c <= "Z":
            val = ord(c) - 55
        elif c == "<":
            val = 0
        else:
            raise ValueError(c)
        total += val * (7, 3, 1)[i % 3]
    return total % 10


# the characters of a German ID card or passport number since 2010/2007: the digits and the consonants
# C F G H J K L M N P R T V W X Y Z (no vowel and no B, D, Q or S, which a reader confuses with 8, 0, O and 5)
DE_DOCUMENT_CHARS = frozenset("CFGHJKLMNPRTVWXYZ" + string.digits)


def de_document_ok(v: str) -> bool:
    """A German ID card or passport number: 9 characters of DE_DOCUMENT_CHARS, as the card prints it, or the same 9
    and the ICAO check digit, as the machine-readable zone prints it. The check digit is not part of the printed number:
    its 9th character can be a letter (the official specimen card's number ends in a digit by chance)."""
    v = v.strip().upper()
    if len(v) not in (9, 10) or any(c not in DE_DOCUMENT_CHARS for c in v[:9]):
        return False
    if len(v) == 10:
        return v[9].isdigit() and icao_check_digit(v[:9]) == int(v[9])
    return True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Presidio --
def pl_pesel_ok(v: str) -> bool:
    """PESEL: weights 1 3 7 9 1 3 7 9 1 3, the check digit is (10 - sum mod 10) mod 10."""
    if len(v) != 11 or not v.isdigit():
        return False
    total = sum(int(d) * w for d, w in zip(v[:10], (1, 3, 7, 9, 1, 3, 7, 9, 1, 3)))
    return (10 - total % 10) % 10 == int(v[10])


_NIF_LETTERS = "TRWAGMYFPDXBNJZSQVHLCKE"


def es_nif_ok(v: str) -> bool:
    """Spanish DNI/NIF: the letter is _NIF_LETTERS[number mod 23] (Presidio removes - and spaces first)."""
    v = re.sub(r"[- ]", "", v.strip()).upper()
    digits = _digits(v)
    return bool(digits) and v[-1:] == _NIF_LETTERS[int(digits) % 23]


def es_nie_ok(v: str) -> bool:
    """Spanish NIE: X, Y or Z (read as 0, 1, 2), the digits, and the NIF letter of that whole number."""
    v = re.sub(r"[- ]", "", v.strip()).upper()
    if not 8 <= len(v) <= 9 or v[:1] not in ("X", "Y", "Z") or not v[1:-1].isdigit():
        return False
    return v[-1] == _NIF_LETTERS[int(str("XYZ".index(v[0])) + v[1:-1]) % 23]


def uk_nhs_ok(v: str) -> bool:
    """NHS number: the ten digits weighted 10 down to 1 sum to a multiple of 11."""
    d = re.sub(r"[- ]", "", v.strip())
    if len(d) != 10 or not d.isdigit() or len(set(d)) == 1:
        return False                    # 000 000 0000 passes the sum and is a placeholder
    return sum(int(c) * w for c, w in zip(d, range(10, 0, -1))) % 11 == 0


def us_aba_ok(v: str) -> bool:
    """ABA routing number: weights 3 7 1, the sum is a multiple of 10, and the first two digits a Federal Reserve
    routing symbol (00 to 12, 21 to 32, 61 to 72, 80). 000000000 passes the sum and is a placeholder."""
    d = re.sub(r"[- ]", "", v.strip())
    if len(d) != 9 or not d.isdigit() or len(set(d)) == 1:
        return False
    head = int(d[:2])
    if not (head <= 12 or 21 <= head <= 32 or 61 <= head <= 72 or head == 80):
        return False
    return sum(int(c) * w for c, w in zip(d, (3, 7, 1) * 3)) % 10 == 0



def us_npi_ok(v: str) -> bool:
    """US NPI (CMS): Luhn over the prefix 80840 and the ten digits; a body of one repeated digit is no number."""
    d = re.sub(r"[- ]", "", v.strip())
    return len(d) == 10 and d.isdigit() and len(set(d[:-1])) > 1 and _luhn_sum("80840" + d) == 0


def se_organisationsnummer_ok(v: str) -> bool:
    """Swedish organisationsnummer: ten digits, the third at least 2, Luhn."""
    d = _digits(v)
    if len(d) == 12 and d.startswith("16"):
        d = d[2:]                        # the century form 16NNNNNN-NNNN of an organisation
    return len(d) == 10 and int(d[2]) >= 2 and _luhn_sum(d) == 0


def uk_vehicle_registration_ok(v: str) -> bool:
